Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any mechanics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 197. The projective resolution has length at most 122 with Betti numbers bounded by 1.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_197(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 197. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^122(S, F) is trivial. QED.
